(1:36:40) specify the solution uh to the problem of whether you like this movie or not this is uh much easier to learn from experience let's be a little bit more concrete so the machine learning tasks are really provided in terms of examples it's very easy to show examples of how you would want uh the method uh to behave examples of correct Behavior I can take an image I can provide an annotated category for that image what I want the method to predict another image corresponding category and so on and now generate a large
(1:37:24) set of examples that illustrates what the task is this is typically very easy to do okay I don't have to specify the solution to be able to uh illustrate the task implicitly through examples the power of machine learning really is now in terms of automating the process of finding the solution to a task that's illustrated by example okay so what we do is we hypothesize a set of possible mappings a large set that takes an image and produce a category as an answer what that mapping is is specified by [Music] parameters a different value for these
(1:38:21) parameters will specify a different mapping and I would like to find out of the possible parameters the one that agrees with my set of examples the best we will automate this process of finding these parameters and also specifying what the mappings are so this is what the machine learning algorithms do for you you can apply the same uh approach in different context text for example in machine translation you can easily specify what the task is by giving an English sentence and a corresponding Spanish sentence and have large number
(1:39:04) of them um to illustrate what the task is then specify a complex mapping that can take a sentence as an input has some adjustable parameters that specify different possible mappings each parameter value provides a possible answer that may or may not agree with the correct one and we search for those parameter values in an automated way uh in order to find the best um uh mapping for this task Google translate already operates uh in this mode now let's take a very concrete example of how to specify a machine learning task and how to solve it
(1:39:54) let's consider a movie recommender problem I have a set of movies I've already seen and I wish to use the experience from those movies to make recommendations of whether I would like to see tens of thousands of other movies first thing to do is to illustrate the task for the learning algorithm so I need to annotate here four Possible movies that I've already seen Fury it was uh too violent I gave it minus one Gone Girl too sad minus one and everything that has to do with science fiction I will give plus one so
(1:40:36) now I have four annotated examples that illustrate the task that I wish to solve how to recommend movies for me the real task though is to recommend correctly movies out of those 10 T of thousands of unseen movies okay there's a here discrepancy between the information available to the algorithm and the task that it wish to solve which is the recommending the future examples that I wish to do accurately we will get to get back to that point now the first step here is that uh while the computer can easily understand
(1:41:22) the plusus one labels I've annotated the movies with it doesn't really understand anything about the movies so I need to construct a feature Vector put the movies into a form that the computers can understand we will compile a feature Vector for each movie systematically so that we can add that procedure apply that procedure to each of the movies in my little uh training set here set of examples that I have already seen scene as well as to any of the future examples so how do we construct the future vectors X here is a vector one
(1:42:02) here uh suqu one denotes uh um the identity of the movie in the uh set of examples that I have 1 2 3 and four now I will construct this feature vector by asking questions about those movies for example I can ask whether it's an action movie and give a binary answer 01 it is an action movie so I give it one is it a comedy no so I give it a zero and so on I can construct many set questions apply them systematically to that movie and as a result get a binary Vector associated with the movie Fury now I can apply the same procedure
(1:42:55) same set of questions in the same order to the other movie gon and construct a feature Vector corresponding to that movie okay I can also apply the same procedure for all of the future movies that I encounter and thereby hopefully make a recommendation for whether I would want to see them or not all right so at this point what we have is a training set of examples vectors with the associated labels these are in the form that computer can Now understand its task is to learn a mapping from those vectors to the labels it's called a classifier as we
